- une lampe | ewn LAHNP | a lamp | —
- un tapis | uhn tah-PEE | a rug, a carpet | —
- un frigo | uhn free-GOH | a fridge | —
- la fenêtre | lah fuh-NEH-truh | the window | —
- faire la lessive | fehr lah leh-SEEV | to do the laundry | —
- faire le lit | fehr luh LEE | to make the bed | —
- passer l'aspirateur | pah-say lahs-pee-rah-TUHR | to vacuum | —
- ranger | rahn-ZHAY | to tidy | —
- sortir les poubelles | sor-teer lay poo-BEL | to take out the bins | —
- nettoyer | neh-twah-YAY | to clean | Use it as an infinitive for now.

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-home-and-chores-ex001 | multiple-choice | Que veut dire faire la cuisine ? | ["to cook","to clean the kitchen","to go to the kitchen"] | ["to cook"] | false | Faire la cuisine means to cook, although la cuisine is also the kitchen.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex002 | fill-in | Je dors dans la ___. (bedroom) | — | ["chambre"] | false | La chambre is the bedroom.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex003 | fill-in | Le frigo est dans la ___. (kitchen) | — | ["cuisine"] | false | La cuisine is the kitchen.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex004 | fill-in | J'habite au deuxième ___. (floor) | — | ["étage"] | false | L'étage is the floor.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex005 | multiple-choice | Which one is correct? | ["une armoire","un armoire","une armoires"] | ["une armoire"] | false | Armoire is feminine and singular here, so it's une armoire.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex006 | fill-in | La lampe est ___ la table. (on) | — | ["sur"] | false | Sur means 'on'.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex007 | fill-in | Le tapis est ___ le lit. (under) | — | ["sous"] | false | Sous means 'under'.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex008 | multiple-choice | How do you say 'to vacuum'? | ["passer l'aspirateur","faire la lessive","sortir les poubelles"] | ["passer l'aspirateur"] | false | Passer l'aspirateur means to vacuum.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex009 | fill-in | Je ___ la vaisselle. (faire) | — | ["fais"] | false | Faire la vaisselle is to do the dishes, and je takes fais.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex010 | translate | Write in French: the living room | — | ["le salon"] | false | Salon is masculine.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex011 | fill-in | Il faut ___ les poubelles. (to take out) | — | ["sortir"] | false | After il faut, the verb stays in the infinitive.
- a2-home-and-chores-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order: I do the laundry on Saturdays. | — | ["Je fais la lessive le samedi","Le samedi je fais la lessive"] | false | Le samedi can go at the end or at the start.

## a2-relative-qui-que-ou

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- qui | kee | who, which, that (the subject) | —
- que / qu' | kuh / k | who, whom, which, that (the object) | —
- où | oo | where, when (after a place or a time) | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex001 | fill-in | C'est la femme ___ travaille ici. | — | ["qui"] | false | The verb follows directly, so it's qui.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex002 | fill-in | C'est le livre ___ je lis. | — | ["que"] | false | A subject, je, follows, so it's que.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex003 | multiple-choice | Choose the word for the blank: C'est le film ___ elle regarde. | ["qu'","qui","où"] | ["qu'"] | false | A subject follows, and que shortens to qu' before the vowel.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex004 | fill-in | C'est la ville ___ il y a un grand musée. | — | ["où"] | true | It refers to a place, and the clause says what's there, so it's où.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex005 | fill-in | C'est le jour ___ nous sommes arrivés. | — | ["où"] | true | It refers to a time, so it's où.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex006 | fill-in | C'est le train ___ part à huit heures. | — | ["qui"] | false | The verb follows directly, so it's qui.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex007 | multiple-choice | Choose the word for the blank: C'est la femme ___ je connais. | ["que","qui","où"] | ["que"] | false | A subject, je, follows, so it's que, even for a person.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex008 | translate | Join these two sentences with a relative pronoun: J'ai un ami. Cet ami habite à Lille. | — | ["J'ai un ami qui habite à Lille"] | true | Cet ami is the subject of habite, so it becomes qui.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex009 | translate | Join these two sentences with a relative pronoun: Voici le livre. J'ai acheté ce livre. | — | ["Voici le livre que j'ai acheté"] | false | Ce livre is the object, so it becomes que. Livre is masculine singular, so acheté doesn't change.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex010 | fill-in | C'est la lettre que j'ai ___. (écrire; la lettre: feminine singular) | — | ["écrite"] | false | Que stands for la lettre, a feminine direct object before the verb, so add -e.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex011 | fill-in | Ce sont les clés que j'ai ___. (prendre; les clés: feminine plural) | — | ["prises"] | false | Que stands for les clés, feminine plural, so it's prises.
- a2-relative-qui-que-ou-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order: It's the house where I live. | — | ["C'est la maison où j'habite"] | false | Où refers to the place.

## a2-describing-your-town

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- le centre-ville | luh sahn-truh VEEL | the town centre | —
- la banlieue | lah bahn-LYUH | the suburbs | —
- le village | luh vee-LAHZH | the village | —
- animé / animée | ah-nee-MAY | lively (masculine / feminine) | —
- propre | PROH-pruh | clean | —
- pratique | prah-TEEK | convenient, practical | —
- pas cher | pah SHEHR | cheap, inexpensive | Fixed phrase for now.
- maintenant | man-tuh-NAHN | now | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-describing-your-town-ex001 | fill-in | Avant, j'___ à Nantes. Maintenant, j'habite à Lille. (habiter, imparfait) | — | ["habitais"] | false | For a place you used to live, use the imparfait.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex002 | fill-in | Le centre-ville est bruyant, mon quartier est très calme. Mon quartier est ___ calme que le centre-ville. | — | ["plus"] | false | The neighbourhood is quieter, so it's plus calme.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex003 | multiple-choice | Choose the words for 'the most': C'est le quartier ___ animé de Lyon. | ["le plus","plus","la plus"] | ["le plus"] | false | Quartier is masculine, and the adjective follows the noun, so the article is repeated.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex004 | fill-in | Voici le village ___ mes parents travaillent. | — | ["où"] | true | It refers to a place, and travailler has no direct object, so it's où.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex005 | multiple-choice | Choose the word for the blank: C'est le quartier ___ j'aime. | ["que","qui","où"] | ["que"] | false | A subject, j', follows, so it's que.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex006 | fill-in | Le centre-ville est ___. (lively; le centre-ville: masculine) | — | ["animé"] | true | Centre-ville is masculine, so there's no added -e.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex007 | multiple-choice | Qu'est-ce que la banlieue ? | ["the suburbs","the town centre","the countryside"] | ["the suburbs"] | false | La banlieue is the area around a big city.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex008 | fill-in | J'habite ici ___ 2020. (and I still live here) | — | ["depuis"] | false | It's still true, so use depuis with the present.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex009 | fill-in | Il y a un marché ___ de chez moi. (near) | — | ["près"] | false | Près de means 'near'.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex010 | translate | Write in French: The village is quieter than the town. | — | ["Le village est plus calme que la ville"] | false | Plus + adjective + que, and calme is the same for masculine and feminine.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex011 | reorder | Put the words in order: It's a neighbourhood that's very quiet. | — | ["C'est un quartier qui est très calme"] | false | The verb follows directly, so it's qui.
- a2-describing-your-town-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order: I live in a flat that's small. | — | ["J'habite dans un appartement qui est petit"] | false | The verb follows directly, so it's qui, and petit matches appartement.

## a2-review-comparing

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- comparer | kohn-pah-RAY | to compare | —
- une différence | ewn dee-fay-RAHNSS | a difference | —
- un avantage | uh-nah-vahn-TAHZH | an advantage | —
- magnifique | mah-nyee-FEEK | magnificent, beautiful | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-review-comparing-ex001 | fill-in | Ma maison a huit pièces, mon appartement a deux pièces. Ma maison est ___ grande que mon appartement. | — | ["plus"] | false | The house is bigger, so it's plus.
- a2-review-comparing-ex002 | multiple-choice | Les fraises are feminine plural. Choose the word for 'better': Ces fraises sont ___ que les autres. | ["meilleures","mieux","plus bonnes"] | ["meilleures"] | false | It describes a noun, so you need the adjective, and it agrees with fraises.
- a2-review-comparing-ex003 | multiple-choice | Choose the word for 'better': Il parle ___ que moi. | ["mieux","meilleur","plus bon"] | ["mieux"] | false | It describes the verb, so you need the adverb.
- a2-review-comparing-ex004 | fill-in | C'est le plus grand musée ___ Paris. (in) | — | ["de"] | false | After a superlative, 'in' is de.
- a2-review-comparing-ex005 | multiple-choice | Choose the word for the blank: C'est le bureau ___ elle travaille. | ["où","qui","que"] | ["où"] | false | It refers to a place, and travailler has no direct object, so it's où.
- a2-review-comparing-ex006 | multiple-choice | Choose the word for the blank: C'est la femme ___ parle. | ["qui","que","où"] | ["qui"] | false | The verb follows directly, so it's qui.
- a2-review-comparing-ex007 | fill-in | Ce sont les lettres que j'ai ___. (écrire; les lettres: feminine plural) | — | ["écrites"] | false | Que stands for les lettres, a feminine plural direct object before the verb.
- a2-review-comparing-ex008 | multiple-choice | Which one means 'to cook'? | ["faire la cuisine","faire la lessive","faire le lit"] | ["faire la cuisine"] | false | Faire la cuisine means to cook, even though la cuisine is also the kitchen.
- a2-review-comparing-ex009 | fill-in | Je fais le ___. (housework) | — | ["ménage"] | false | Faire le ménage means to do the housework.
- a2-review-comparing-ex010 | translate | Write out this paragraph, filling each gap as shown: Ma maison est (more + grand; ma maison: feminine) que ton appartement. Ton appartement est (less + bruyant; ton appartement: masculine). Lille est (the best + ville; in: de) la région. | — | ["Ma maison est plus grande que ton appartement. Ton appartement est moins bruyant. Lille est la meilleure ville de la région."] | false | Each adjective agrees with its noun, and after a superlative 'in' is de.
- a2-review-comparing-ex011 | reorder | Put the words in order: Here's the house I bought. | — | ["Voici la maison que j'ai achetée"] | false | Que stands for la maison, feminine, so the participle adds -e.
- a2-review-comparing-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order (the subject is Marie): She's taller than me. | — | ["Elle est plus grande que moi"] | false | Grande agrees with Marie, and a stressed pronoun follows que.

## a2-emotions-and-feelings

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- triste | TREEST | sad | Same for masculine and feminine. Plural: tristes.
- fâché / fâchée | fah-SHAY | annoyed, cross (masculine / feminine) | Plural: fâchés / fâchées.
- inquiet / inquiète | an-KYEH / an-KYET | worried (masculine / feminine) | Plural: inquiets / inquiètes.
- jaloux / jalouse | zhah-LOO / zhah-LOOZ | jealous (masculine / feminine) | Plural: jaloux / jalouses.
- fier / fière | fyehr | proud (masculine / feminine) | Plural: fiers / fières.
- déçu / déçue | day-SEW | disappointed (masculine / feminine) | Plural: déçus / déçues.
- surpris / surprise | sewr-PREE / sewr-PREEZ | surprised (masculine / feminine) | Plural: surpris / surprises.
- énervé / énervée | ay-nehr-VAY | irritated, on edge (masculine / feminine) | Plural: énervés / énervées.
- stressé / stressée | streh-SAY | stressed (masculine / feminine) | Plural: stressés / stressées.
- en colère | ahn koh-LEHR | angry | Never changes.
- de bonne humeur | duh bonn ew-MUHR | in a good mood | Never changes.
- de mauvaise humeur | duh moh-vehz ew-MUHR | in a bad mood | Never changes.
- Ça me fait plaisir. | sah muh feh pleh-ZEER | That makes me happy. I'm pleased. | —
- Je suis content de te voir. | zhuh swee kohn-tahn duh tuh VWAHR | I'm happy to see you. | A woman says: Je suis contente de te voir.
- Je suis désolé de… | zhuh swee day-zoh-LAY duh | I'm sorry to…, I'm sorry for… | A woman writes désolée. Followed by an infinitive.
- Qu'est-ce que tu as ? | kes-kuh tew AH | What's the matter with you? | —
- Qu'est-ce qu'il y a ? | kes-keel YAH | What's wrong? What's going on? | —
- Ça ne va pas ? | sah nuh vah PAH | Is something wrong? Aren't you OK? | —
- une fleur | ewn FLUHR | a flower | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex001 | fill-in | Marie (a woman) est ___ aujourd'hui. (content) | — | ["contente"] | false | Marie is feminine, so content adds -e.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex002 | fill-in | Paul et Léo (two men) sont ___. (heureux) | — | ["heureux"] | false | A masculine adjective ending in -x stays the same in the plural.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex003 | fill-in | Anne (a woman) est ___ pour son fils. (inquiet) | — | ["inquiète"] | false | The feminine adds -e and takes a grave accent: inquiète.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex004 | multiple-choice | Léa is a woman. Choose the right form: Léa est ___ de sa sœur. | ["jalouse","jaloux","jalouses"] | ["jalouse"] | false | Léa is one woman, so it's the feminine singular.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex005 | fill-in | Ma mère (a woman) est ___ de moi. (fier) | — | ["fière"] | false | The feminine of fier is fière, with a grave accent.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex006 | fill-in | Mes sœurs (two women) sont ___. (déçu) | — | ["déçues"] | false | Feminine plural: add -e and then -s.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex007 | multiple-choice | Marie is a woman. Choose the right form: Marie est ___. | ["en colère","en colères","en colèrée"] | ["en colère"] | false | En colère never changes.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex008 | fill-in | J'ai ___ des chiens. (fear) | — | ["peur"] | false | French says 'I have fear': avoir peur de.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex009 | multiple-choice | Your friend looks upset. What do you ask? | ["Qu'est-ce que tu as ?","Tu as quel âge ?","Tu t'appelles comment ?"] | ["Qu'est-ce que tu as ?"] | false | Qu'est-ce que tu as ? asks what's the matter.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex010 | fill-in | Merci pour le cadeau, ça me fait ___ ! | — | ["plaisir"] | false | Ça me fait plaisir is a fixed phrase.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex011 | translate | Write in French (the speaker is a man, talking to a friend): I'm happy to see you. | — | ["Je suis content de te voir"] | false | Content matches the man speaking, and te comes before voir.
- a2-emotions-and-feelings-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order (the subject is Marie): She's a bit sad today. | — | ["Elle est un peu triste aujourd'hui","Aujourd'hui elle est un peu triste"] | false | Un peu comes before the adjective. Aujourd'hui can go at either end.

## a2-imperative-with-pronouns

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- Vas-y ! / Allez-y ! | vah ZEE / ah-lay ZEE | Go on! Go ahead! (tu / vous) | —
- Allons-y ! | ah-lohn ZEE | Let's go! | —
- Prends-en. | prahn ZAHN | Take some. | —
- Manges-en. | mahnzh ZAHN | Eat some. | —
- Penses-y. | pahns ZEE | Think about it. | —
- Donne-le-moi. | donn luh MWAH | Give it to me. | —
- Dis-le-moi. | dee luh MWAH | Tell me. | —
- Montre-le-moi. | mohn-truh luh MWAH | Show it to me. | —
- Passe-le-lui. | pahs luh LWEE | Pass it to him / to her. | —
- Lève-toi. / Levez-vous. | lev TWAH / luh-vay VOO | Get up. (tu / vous) | —
- Dépêche-toi. / Dépêchez-vous. | day-pesh TWAH / day-peh-shay VOO | Hurry up. (tu / vous) | —
- Dépêchons-nous. | day-peh-shohn NOO | Let's hurry. | —
- Habille-toi. | ah-bee-y TWAH | Get dressed. | —
- Reposez-vous. | ruh-poh-zay VOO | Have a rest. | —
- Ne t'inquiète pas. / Ne vous inquiétez pas. | nuh tan-KYET pah / nuh voo zan-kyay-tay PAH | Don't worry. (tu / vous) | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex001 | fill-in | Speaking to a friend (tu), replacing le film: Regarde le film. → Regarde-___ ! | — | ["le"] | false | Le film is masculine and a direct object, so it's le, after the verb.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex002 | translate | Build the command, speaking to your boss (vous), replacing à Marie with a pronoun: Téléphonez à Marie. | — | ["Téléphonez-lui"] | false | À Marie becomes lui, which goes after the verb with a hyphen.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex003 | fill-in | Speaking to a friend (tu), saying 'Look at me!': Regarde-___ ! | — | ["moi"] | false | After the verb, me becomes moi.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex004 | fill-in | Speaking to a friend (tu), replacing à la gare with y: Va à la gare. → ___-y ! | — | ["Vas"] | false | The s comes back before y.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex005 | multiple-choice | Speaking to a friend (tu), replacing du gâteau: Mange du gâteau. Which one is correct? | ["Manges-en.","Mange-en.","En mange."] | ["Manges-en."] | false | The s of the tu form comes back before en.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex006 | translate | Build the negative command, speaking to a friend (tu), replacing le gâteau with a pronoun: Ne mange pas le gâteau. | — | ["Ne le mange pas"] | true | In the negative, the pronoun goes back before the verb.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex007 | multiple-choice | Speaking to a friend (tu): Don't call me. | ["Ne m'appelle pas.","N'appelle-moi pas.","Ne appelle pas moi."] | ["Ne m'appelle pas."] | false | In the negative, the pronoun goes before the verb, and me stays as m'.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex008 | fill-in | Speaking to your son (tu), telling him to get up: ___-toi, il est tard ! (se lever) | — | ["Lève"] | false | The tu form of an -er verb has no s, and toi follows it.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex009 | multiple-choice | Speaking to a friend (tu): Don't worry. | ["Ne t'inquiète pas.","Ne toi inquiète pas.","Inquiète-toi pas."] | ["Ne t'inquiète pas."] | false | In the negative, the reflexive pronoun is te, and it goes before the verb.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex010 | fill-in | Speaking to a friend (tu), replacing le livre, with the pronouns in the usual order: Donne-___-moi. | — | ["le"] | false | In this pattern, le, la or les comes first.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex011 | reorder | Put the words in order, speaking to a friend (tu) about the keys: Take them and don't forget them. | — | ["Prends-les et ne les oublie pas"] | false | The affirmative has the pronoun after the verb, and the negative has it before.
- a2-imperative-with-pronouns-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order, speaking to a friend (tu): Don't get up this morning. | — | ["Ne te lève pas ce matin","Ce matin ne te lève pas"] | false | Te stays before the verb in the negative.

fr | pron | en | note

- commencer à | koh-mahn-SAY ah | to start to, to start …ing | —
- apprendre à | ah-PRAHN-druh ah | to learn to | —
- réussir à | ray-ew-SEER ah | to manage to, to succeed in | —
- aider quelqu'un à | eh-day kel-KUHN ah | to help someone to | —
- continuer à | kohn-tee-new-AY ah | to carry on …ing | —
- essayer de | eh-seh-YAY duh | to try to | —
- décider de | day-see-DAY duh | to decide to | —
- arrêter de | ah-reh-TAY duh | to stop …ing | —
- finir de | fee-NEER duh | to finish …ing | —
- oublier de | oo-blee-AY duh | to forget to | —
- refuser de | ruh-few-ZAY duh | to refuse to | —
- j'essaie / j'essaye | zheh-SEH / zheh-SEH-y | I try | Both spellings are correct.
- nous essayons | noo zeh-seh-YOHN | we try | —
- nous commençons | noo koh-mahn-SOHN | we start | —
- fermer | fehr-MAY | to close | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex001 | fill-in | J'apprends ___ nager. | — | ["à"] | true | Apprendre takes à before an infinitive.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex002 | fill-in | Paul a réussi ___ finir son travail. | — | ["à"] | true | Réussir takes à.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex003 | multiple-choice | Choose what goes in the gap: Je l'aide ___ ranger la cuisine. | ["à","de","no word"] | ["à"] | false | Aider quelqu'un à: the person comes first, then à.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex004 | fill-in | J'essaie ___ parler français. | — | ["de"] | false | Essayer takes de.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex005 | multiple-choice | Choose what goes in the gap: Elle a décidé ___ partir. | ["de","à","no word"] | ["de"] | false | Décider takes de.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex006 | fill-in | Arrête ___ parler ! | — | ["de"] | false | Arrêter takes de.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex007 | fill-in | J'ai oublié ___ téléphoner à Anne. | — | ["de"] | false | Oublier takes de.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex008 | multiple-choice | Choose what goes in the gap: Je veux ___ voyager. | ["no word","à","de"] | ["no word"] | false | Vouloir goes straight to the infinitive.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex009 | fill-in | Le matin, nous ___ à huit heures. (commencer, present) | — | ["commençons"] | false | Before o, the c takes a cedilla.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex010 | multiple-choice | Choose what goes in the gap: J'apprends ___ français. | ["le","à","de"] | ["le"] | false | With a noun, apprendre needs no preposition, only the article.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex011 | translate | Write in French: She refused to answer. | — | ["Elle a refusé de répondre"] | true | Refuser takes de. The participle comes before de and the infinitive.
- a2-verbs-with-a-and-de-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order: I started working at eight o'clock. | — | ["J'ai commencé à travailler à huit heures","À huit heures j'ai commencé à travailler"] | false | Commencer à + infinitive. The time can go at either end.

## a2-polite-requests

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- Tu peux… ? | tew PUH | Can you…? (informal) | —
- Tu pourrais… ? | tew poo-REH | Could you…? (informal) | A fixed phrase for now.
- Vous pouvez… ? | voo poo-VAY | Can you…? (formal) | —
- Pourriez-vous… ? | poo-ryay VOO | Could you…? (formal) | A fixed phrase for now.
- Est-ce que vous pourriez… ? | es-kuh voo poo-RYAY | Could you…? (formal) | A fixed phrase for now.
- Je pourrais avoir… ? | zhuh poo-reh ah-VWAHR | Could I have…? | A fixed phrase for now.
- Merci de… | mehr-see DUH | Please… (on signs and in writing) | Followed by an infinitive.
- Excusez-moi de vous déranger. | eks-kew-zay mwah duh voo day-rahn-ZHAY | Sorry to bother you. | —
- Pas de problème. | pah duh proh-BLEM | No problem. | —
- indiquer | an-dee-KAY | to show, to point out (the way) | —
- apporter | ah-por-TAY | to bring | —
- le sel | luh SEL | the salt | —
- Tiens. / Tenez. | tyan / tuh-NAY | Here you are. (tu / vous) | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-polite-requests-ex001 | multiple-choice | You stop a stranger in the street and want to be very polite. Which one fits? | ["Pourriez-vous m'indiquer la gare ?","Tu peux m'indiquer la gare ?","Indique-moi la gare."] | ["Pourriez-vous m'indiquer la gare ?"] | false | With a stranger, use vous and a polite question.
- a2-polite-requests-ex002 | multiple-choice | You're at home, asking your brother for help. Which one fits? | ["Tu peux m'aider ?","Pourriez-vous m'aider ?","Est-ce que vous pourriez m'aider ?"] | ["Tu peux m'aider ?"] | false | Your brother is someone you call tu.
- a2-polite-requests-ex003 | multiple-choice | You'd like the bill from the waiter, and you want to be as polite as you can. Which one fits? | ["Pourriez-vous m'apporter l'addition ?","Donnez-moi l'addition.","Apporte-moi l'addition."] | ["Pourriez-vous m'apporter l'addition ?"] | false | A bare command is too abrupt, and a waiter is someone you call vous.
- a2-polite-requests-ex004 | multiple-choice | A sign on a door says: Merci de fermer la porte. What does it mean? | ["Please close the door.","The door is closed.","Don't close the door."] | ["Please close the door."] | false | Merci de + infinitive is a polite instruction on signs and in writing.
- a2-polite-requests-ex005 | fill-in | ___-vous répéter, s'il vous plaît ? (could, very polite) | — | ["Pourriez"] | false | Pourriez-vous is the polite fixed phrase.
- a2-polite-requests-ex006 | fill-in | Tu ___ m'aider ? (can, present tense) | — | ["peux"] | false | With tu, pouvoir is peux in the present.
- a2-polite-requests-ex007 | multiple-choice | A customer you call vous says Merci beaucoup ! Which reply fits? | ["Je vous en prie.","Excusez-moi.","Pardon."] | ["Je vous en prie."] | false | Je vous en prie is the formal answer to thanks.
- a2-polite-requests-ex008 | fill-in | Je ___ une chambre pour deux nuits. (I'd like) | — | ["voudrais"] | false | Je voudrais is the polite way of saying what you want.
- a2-polite-requests-ex009 | translate | Write in French, speaking to a friend (tu), with the present tense of pouvoir: Can you help me? | — | ["Tu peux m'aider","Est-ce que tu peux m'aider","Peux-tu m'aider"] | false | Tu peux + infinitive, with m' before aider.
- a2-polite-requests-ex010 | translate | Write in French, speaking to a stranger (vous), starting with Pourriez-vous: Could you speak more slowly? | — | ["Pourriez-vous parler plus lentement"] | false | Pourriez-vous + infinitive, and plus lentement from the adverbs lesson.
- a2-polite-requests-ex011 | reorder | Put the words in order: Could you speak more slowly? (formal) | — | ["Est-ce que vous pourriez parler plus lentement"] | false | Est-ce que + vous pourriez + infinitive.
- a2-polite-requests-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order: Sorry to bother you. | — | ["Excusez-moi de vous déranger"] | false | A fixed phrase, with vous before déranger.

## a2-writing-an-informal-email

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- À très vite ! | ah treh VEET | Talk soon! See you very soon! | —
- Bises | beez | Love (to friends and family) | —
- Je t'embrasse | zhuh tahn-BRAHSS | Love, big hug (to friends and family) | —
- Écris-moi vite ! | ay-kree mwah VEET | Write back soon! | —
- Merci pour ton message. | mehr-see poor tohn meh-SAHZH | Thanks for your message. | —
- Comment vas-tu ? | koh-mahn vah TEW | How are you? | —
- J'espère que tu vas bien. | zheh-spehr kuh tew vah BYAN | I hope you're well. | —
- Je t'écris pour… | zhuh tay-KREE poor | I'm writing to… | Followed by an infinitive.
- donner des nouvelles | doh-nay day noo-VEL | to give (someone) your news | —
- Dis bonjour à … de ma part. | dee bohn-ZHOOR ah … duh mah PAR | Say hello to … from me. | —
- Tu me manques. | tew muh MAHNK | I miss you. | —
- P.-S. | pay ESS | P.S. | —
- alors | ah-LOR | so, then | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex001 | multiple-choice | You're writing to your best friend Julie. Which closing fits? | ["Grosses bises","Cordialement","Excusez-moi"] | ["Grosses bises"] | false | Grosses bises is for friends and family.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex002 | multiple-choice | You're writing to your teacher, whom you call vous. Which closing fits? | ["Cordialement","Je t'embrasse","Bises"] | ["Cordialement"] | false | Cordialement is neutral. The others are for people you're close to.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex003 | multiple-choice | Your friend Anne is a woman. Choose the right opening. | ["Chère Anne,","Cher Anne,","Chers Anne,"] | ["Chère Anne,"] | false | Anne is one woman, so it's chère.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex004 | fill-in | Merci ___ ton message. | — | ["pour"] | false | Merci pour + noun.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex005 | fill-in | J'espère que tu ___ bien. (aller) | — | ["vas"] | false | With tu, aller is vas.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex006 | fill-in | Il ___ beau quand Paul est arrivé à Nice. (faire, imparfait: the scene) | — | ["faisait"] | false | The imparfait sets the scene.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex007 | fill-in | Hier, j'___ Marc en ville. (voir, passé composé) | — | ["ai vu"] | false | A single event in the past takes the passé composé.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex008 | fill-in | L'été prochain, j'___ à Lyon. (aller, futur simple) | — | ["irai"] | false | The future stem of aller is ir-.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex009 | translate | Write in French, to a friend: I miss you. | — | ["Tu me manques"] | false | In French, the person you miss is the subject.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex010 | reorder | Put the parts of this email in a logical order. | — | ["Salut Léa ! Merci pour ton message. Hier, je suis allé au cinéma avec Marc. Le week-end prochain, je vais visiter Rouen. Et toi, comment vas-tu ? Bises, Paul"] | false | Greeting, thanks, past news, future plans, a question back, then the closing.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex011 | reorder | Put these sentences in a logical order (the writer is a man). | — | ["Il pleuvait quand je suis sorti. Alors, je suis rentré à la maison. Demain, il fera beau."] | false | The scene, then what happened, then what comes next.
- a2-writing-an-informal-email-ex012 | translate | Write out this email, filling each gap as shown: Salut Léa ! Merci (for) ton message. Hier, je (aller, passé composé; je: a woman) à la plage. Il (faire, imparfait) beau. Demain, je (travailler, futur simple). Et toi, comment (aller + tu, with inversion) ? Bises | — | ["Salut Léa ! Merci pour ton message. Hier, je suis allée à la plage. Il faisait beau. Demain, je travaillerai. Et toi, comment vas-tu ? Bises"] | true | Aller takes être and agrees with a woman, the imparfait sets the scene, and the future ends in -ai with je.

## a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals

fr | pron | en | note

- le sapin | luh sah-PAN | the Christmas tree, the fir tree | —

- le Nouvel An | luh noo-vel AHN | New Year | —

- la fève | lah FEV | the small charm hidden in the cake | —

- la Chandeleur | lah shahn-duh-LUHR | Candlemas, the pancake day | —
- une crêpe | ewn KREP | a crêpe, a thin pancake | —
- couper | koo-PAY | to cut | —
- Pâques | PAHK | Easter | —
- la fête nationale | lah fet nah-syoh-NAHL | the national holiday | —
- un défilé | uhn day-fee-LAY | a parade | —
- un feu d'artifice | uhn fuh dar-tee-FEESS | a firework display | Plural: des feux d'artifice.
- la Fête de la musique | lah fet duh lah mew-ZEEK | the music festival held across France | —
- fêter | feh-TAY | to celebrate | —
- un jour férié | uhn zhoor fay-RYAY | a public holiday | —
- un repas de famille | uhn ruh-pah duh fah-MEE-y | a family meal | —
- Joyeux Noël ! | zhwah-yuh noh-EL | Merry Christmas! | —
- Bonne année ! | bonn ah-NAY | Happy New Year! | —
- Bonnes fêtes ! | bonn FET | Happy holidays! | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex001 | multiple-choice | Qu'est-ce qu'on fête le 14 juillet ? | ["la fête nationale","la Chandeleur","Pâques"] | ["la fête nationale"] | false | Le 14 juillet is the national holiday.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex002 | multiple-choice | On mange la bûche à quelle occasion ? | ["à Noël","le 14 juillet","le 1er mai"] | ["à Noël"] | false | La bûche is a traditional Christmas cake.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex003 | multiple-choice | C'est quand, la Fête de la musique ? | ["le 21 juin","le 1er mai","le 25 décembre"] | ["le 21 juin"] | false | La Fête de la musique is on 21 June.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex004 | multiple-choice | Pour quelle fête mange-t-on des crêpes ? | ["la Chandeleur","la fête nationale","la Fête de la musique"] | ["la Chandeleur"] | false | Crêpes are the traditional food of la Chandeleur.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex005 | multiple-choice | It's 1 January. What do you say? | ["Bonne année !","Joyeux Noël !","Bon voyage !"] | ["Bonne année !"] | false | Bonne année is the New Year greeting.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex006 | multiple-choice | It's 25 December. What do you say? | ["Joyeux Noël !","Bonne année !","Bon anniversaire !"] | ["Joyeux Noël !"] | false | Joyeux Noël is the Christmas greeting.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex007 | fill-in | Dans la galette des rois, il y a une ___. (the hidden charm) | — | ["fève"] | false | La fève is the small charm hidden in the cake.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex008 | fill-in | La Fête des Lumières, c'est ___ Lyon. | — | ["à"] | true | Use à with a city.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex009 | fill-in | Le 14 juillet, on regarde les feux d'___. | — | ["artifice"] | false | Des feux d'artifice are fireworks.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex010 | fill-in | Quand j'étais enfant, on ___ une galette en janvier. (manger, imparfait) | — | ["mangeait"] | false | A repeated habit in the past takes the imparfait, and manger keeps its e before a.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex011 | fill-in | L'année dernière, nous avons ___ le 14 juillet à Paris. (fêter) | — | ["fêté"] | true | The participle of fêter is fêté.
- a2-culture-holidays-and-festivals-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order: We give presents at Christmas. | — | ["On offre des cadeaux à Noël","À Noël on offre des cadeaux"] | false | On + offre, and à Noël can go at either end.

## a2-review-final

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- faire des progrès | fehr day proh-GREH | to make progress | —
- un objectif | uh-nob-zhek-TEEF | a goal | —
- la suite | lah SWEET | what comes next | —
- un concert | uhn kohn-SEHR | a concert | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- a2-review-final-ex001 | fill-in | Hier, Marie (a woman) ___ allée au marché. (être or avoir) | — | ["est"] | false | Aller takes être in the passé composé.
- a2-review-final-ex002 | fill-in | Nous avons ___ le film. (voir) | — | ["vu"] | false | The participle of voir is vu.
- a2-review-final-ex003 | multiple-choice | Choose the right form (a repeated habit): Quand j'étais petit, je ___ au foot tous les samedis. | ["jouais","ai joué","jouerai"] | ["jouais"] | false | Tous les samedis describes a habit in the past, so it's the imparfait.
- a2-review-final-ex004 | multiple-choice | Your brother is a doctor. Mon frère ? ___ médecin. | ["Il est","C'est","Il a"] | ["Il est"] | false | With a job and no article, use il est.
- a2-review-final-ex005 | fill-in | J'habite ici ___ trois ans. (and I still live here) | — | ["depuis"] | false | It's still going on, so use depuis with the present.
- a2-review-final-ex006 | multiple-choice | Tu vois Paul ? Choose the pronoun: Oui, je ___ vois. | ["le","lui","y"] | ["le"] | false | Paul is the direct object of voir, so it's le.
- a2-review-final-ex007 | fill-in | J'habite ___ France. | — | ["en"] | false | France is feminine, so it takes en.
- a2-review-final-ex008 | fill-in | Paul a 30 ans, Marc a 40 ans. Paul est ___ âgé que Marc. | — | ["moins"] | false | Paul is younger, so he's less old than Marc.
- a2-review-final-ex009 | translate | Build the command, speaking to a friend (tu), replacing le livre with a pronoun: Prends le livre. | — | ["Prends-le"] | false | In a positive command, the pronoun follows the verb with a hyphen.
- a2-review-final-ex010 | translate | Write out this paragraph, filling each gap as shown: Hier, je (aller, passé composé; je: a man) à Lyon. Il (pleuvoir, imparfait). Cette ville est (more + beau; la ville: feminine) que Dijon. Demain, je (rentrer, futur simple). | — | ["Hier, je suis allé à Lyon. Il pleuvait. Cette ville est plus belle que Dijon. Demain, je rentrerai."] | true | Aller takes être, the imparfait sets the scene, belle agrees with ville, and rentrer takes -ai with je.
- a2-review-final-ex011 | reorder | Put the words in order (the object is Paul): I didn't see him. | — | ["Je ne l'ai pas vu"] | false | The pronoun goes before the auxiliary, and ne…pas goes around them.
- a2-review-final-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order: I phoned her yesterday. | — | ["Je lui ai téléphoné hier","Hier je lui ai téléphoné"] | false | Téléphoner à takes an indirect pronoun, so there's no agreement.

## b1-expressing-opinions

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- à mon avis | ah moh-nah-VEE | in my opinion | —
- selon moi | suh-lohn MWAH | in my view | —
- d'après moi | dah-preh MWAH | as I see it | —
- à vrai dire | ah vreh DEER | to tell the truth | —
- franchement | frahnsh-MAHN | honestly, frankly | —
- penser que | pahn-SAY kuh | to think that | —
- croire que | KRWAHR kuh | to believe that, to think that | Je crois, tu crois, il croit, nous croyons, vous croyez, ils croient.
- trouver que | troo-VAY kuh | to find that, to think that | —
- Qu'est-ce que tu en penses ? | kess-kuh tew ahn PAHNSS | What do you think of it? | With vous: Qu'est-ce que vous en pensez ?
- Tu trouves ? | tew TROOV | Do you think so? | —
- Ça dépend. | sah day-PAHN | It depends. | —
- un avis | uh-nah-VEE | an opinion | —
- un point de vue | uhn pwan duh VEW | a point of view | —
- original / originale | oh-ree-zhee-NAHL | original, unusual | —
- ennuyeux / ennuyeuse | ahn-nwee-YUH / ahn-nwee-YUHZ | boring | —
- génial / géniale | zhay-NYAHL | great, brilliant | —
- une série | ewn say-REE | a series, a TV series | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-expressing-opinions-ex001 | multiple-choice | Which phrase introduces an opinion? | ["À mon avis","À bientôt","À côté de"] | ["À mon avis"] | false | À mon avis means 'in my opinion'. À bientôt is a goodbye, and à côté de means 'next to'.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex002 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (in my opinion): ___ mon avis, ce film est trop long. | — | ["À"] | true | The phrase is à mon avis, with à.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex003 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: Je pense ___ Lyon est plus calme que Paris. | — | ["que"] | false | Penser needs que before a whole opinion. English can drop 'that', but French can't drop que.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex004 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (in my view): Selon ___, la série est géniale. | — | ["moi"] | false | Selon moi uses the stressed pronoun moi.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex005 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with croire in the present: Je ___ que le magasin ferme à 19 heures. | — | ["crois"] | false | Croire is irregular: je crois, tu crois, il croit.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex006 | multiple-choice | La série is feminine. Choose the right form: Je trouve cette série ___. | ["géniale","génial","géniaux"] | ["géniale"] | false | After trouver + noun, the adjective agrees with the noun: série is feminine, so géniale.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex007 | multiple-choice | Which sentence is correct? | ["Je pense que c'est bien.","Je pense c'est bien.","Je pense de c'est bien."] | ["Je pense que c'est bien."] | false | A whole opinion after penser always starts with que.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex008 | multiple-choice | You want to ask a friend what they think of a film. What do you say? | ["Qu'est-ce que tu en penses ?","Qu'est-ce que tu fais ?","Où est le cinéma ?"] | ["Qu'est-ce que tu en penses ?"] | false | Qu'est-ce que tu en penses ? asks for an opinion. En stands for 'of it'.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex009 | multiple-choice | You can't give a clear answer, because it depends on the price. What do you say? | ["Ça dépend du prix.","À vrai dire, merci.","Pour moi, un café."] | ["Ça dépend du prix."] | false | Dépendre takes de, and de + le becomes du.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex010 | translate | Write in French: In my opinion, the train is more practical than the car. | — | ["À mon avis, le train est plus pratique que la voiture"] | true | Start with à mon avis, then use plus … que to compare.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex011 | translate | Write in French: Honestly, I find this film boring. | — | ["Franchement, je trouve ce film ennuyeux","Franchement, je trouve que ce film est ennuyeux"] | false | Trouver works with a noun and an adjective, or with que and a whole sentence.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order: I think this café is too expensive. | — | ["Je pense que ce café est trop cher"] | false | Je pense que, then an ordinary sentence.
- b1-expressing-opinions-ex013 | translate | Write out this paragraph about a town, filling each gap as shown: (In my opinion) Lyon est une belle ville. (I find that) les gens sont sympas. (For me) c'est la meilleure ville de la région. | — | ["À mon avis, Lyon est une belle ville. Je trouve que les gens sont sympas. Pour moi, c'est la meilleure ville de la région."] | true | Three different markers: à mon avis, je trouve que and pour moi.

## b1-agreeing-disagreeing

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- être d'accord (avec) | eh-truh dah-KOR (ah-VEK) | to agree (with) | —
- tout à fait | too-tah-FEH | absolutely, quite | —
- absolument | ahb-soh-lew-MAHN | absolutely | —
- exactement | eg-zak-tuh-MAHN | exactly | —
- c'est vrai | seh VREH | that's true | —
- je suis du même avis | zhuh swee dew meh-mah-VEE | I see it the same way | —
- pas du tout | pah dew TOO | not at all | —
- au contraire | oh kohn-TREHR | on the contrary | —
- un désaccord | uhn day-zah-KOR | a disagreement | —
- avoir raison | ah-vwahr reh-ZOHN | to be right | —
- avoir tort | ah-vwahr TOR | to be wrong | —
- ce n'est pas faux | suh neh pah FOH | that's a fair point | —
- en partie | ahn par-TEE | partly | —
- d'un côté… de l'autre | duhn koh-TAY … duh LOH-truh | on the one hand… on the other | —
- je ne suis pas sûr / sûre | zhuh nuh swee pah SEWR | I'm not sure | A woman writes sûre.
- moi si | mwah SEE | I do, I am (contradicting a negative) | —
- ouvrir | oo-VREER | to open | J'ouvre, tu ouvres, il ouvre, nous ouvrons, vous ouvrez, ils ouvrent.
- seul / seule | SUHL | only, alone | Le seul jour: the only day.

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex001 | multiple-choice | Your friend says the film was great, and you think so too. What do you say? | ["Je suis d'accord avec toi.","Je ne suis pas d'accord.","Pas du tout."] | ["Je suis d'accord avec toi."] | false | Être d'accord avec quelqu'un means to agree with them.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex002 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (You're right): Tu as ___. Ce restaurant est très bon. | — | ["raison"] | false | To be right is avoir raison, with avoir.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex003 | multiple-choice | Choose the right verb: Vous ___ tort. | ["avez","êtes","faites"] | ["avez"] | false | Avoir tort, like avoir raison, uses avoir.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex004 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (I don't agree with you): Je ne suis pas ___ avec toi. | — | ["d'accord"] | false | Être d'accord goes into the negative like any être sentence: je ne suis pas d'accord.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex005 | multiple-choice | Someone says « Je n'aime pas le café. » You do like coffee. What do you say? | ["Moi si.","Moi aussi.","Moi non plus."] | ["Moi si."] | false | Moi si contradicts a negative statement.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex006 | multiple-choice | Someone says « Je n'aime pas le café. » You don't like it either. What do you say? | ["Moi non plus.","Moi aussi.","Moi si."] | ["Moi non plus."] | false | Moi non plus agrees with a negative statement.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex007 | multiple-choice | Which reply politely accepts part of what someone said? | ["Ce n'est pas faux.","Tu as tort.","Pas du tout."] | ["Ce n'est pas faux."] | false | Ce n'est pas faux (that's a fair point) concedes a point without agreeing completely.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex008 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (on the other): D'un côté, c'est pratique. De ___, c'est cher. | — | ["l'autre"] | false | D'un côté… de l'autre balances two sides.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex009 | multiple-choice | Which one is the strongest disagreement? | ["Pas du tout !","En partie.","Ce n'est pas faux."] | ["Pas du tout !"] | false | Pas du tout is a firm 'not at all'. The other two only half disagree.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex010 | translate | Write in French, to a friend: I don't agree with you at all. | — | ["Je ne suis pas du tout d'accord avec toi"] | false | Du tout goes straight after pas: je ne suis pas du tout d'accord.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex011 | translate | Write in French, to your manager (vous): You're right, it's true. | — | ["Vous avez raison, c'est vrai"] | false | Avoir raison, with vous avez.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex012 | reorder | Put the words in order (a woman speaking): I'm not sure, but I partly agree. | — | ["Je ne suis pas sûre, mais je suis en partie d'accord"] | false | A woman writes sûre. En partie goes between suis and d'accord.
- b1-agreeing-disagreeing-ex013 | translate | Write out this forum reply, filling each gap as shown: (That's a fair point), mais (in my opinion) les magasins doivent fermer le dimanche. (On the one hand) c'est pratique, (on the other) les vendeurs ont besoin de se reposer. | — | ["Ce n'est pas faux, mais à mon avis les magasins doivent fermer le dimanche. D'un côté c'est pratique, de l'autre les vendeurs ont besoin de se reposer."] | true | Concede first with ce n'est pas faux, then give your opinion, and balance the two sides.

## b1-ce-qui-ce-que

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- ce qui | suh KEE | what (as subject) | —
- ce que / ce qu' | suh KUH / suh K | what (as object) | —
- tout ce qui | too suh KEE | everything that (as subject) | —
- tout ce que | too suh KUH | everything that (as object) | —
- Ce qui me plaît, c'est… | suh kee muh PLEH, seh | What I like is… | —
- Ce qui compte, c'est… | suh kee KOHNT, seh | What matters is… | —
- plaire à | PLEHR ah | to please, to appeal to | Ça me plaît (I like it). Il plaît takes a circumflex.
- intéresser | an-tay-reh-SAY | to interest | —
- énerver | ay-nehr-VAY | to annoy | —
- choquer | shoh-KAY | to shock | —
- gêner | zheh-NAY | to bother | —
- surprendre | sewr-PRAHN-druh | to surprise | Like prendre: ça me surprend.
- la vérité | lah vay-ree-TAY | the truth | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex001 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (What): ___ me plaît, c'est la musique. | — | ["Ce qui"] | false | A verb follows (after the pronoun me), so it's ce qui.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex002 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (What): ___ j'aime, c'est le dimanche. | — | ["Ce que"] | false | A subject (j') follows, so it's ce que.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex003 | multiple-choice | Choose the right word: Je ne comprends pas ___ tu dis. | ["ce que","ce qui","qu'est-ce que"] | ["ce que"] | false | Tu is the subject of dis, so it's ce que. Qu'est-ce que only starts a question.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex004 | multiple-choice | Choose the right word: ___ m'énerve, c'est la pluie. | ["Ce qui","Ce que","Qui"] | ["Ce qui"] | false | The thing that annoys is the subject of énerve, so it's ce qui.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex005 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (everything): Tout ___ tu dis est vrai. | — | ["ce que"] | false | Tu is the subject of dis, so it's tout ce que.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex006 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (que, before a vowel): Ce ___ il veut, c'est dormir. | — | ["qu'"] | false | Que becomes qu' before a vowel: ce qu'il veut.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex007 | multiple-choice | Choose the right word: Tout ___ est sur la table est pour toi. | ["ce qui","ce que","que"] | ["ce qui"] | false | Est follows straight away, so it's tout ce qui.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex008 | multiple-choice | Which sentence is correct? | ["J'aime ce que tu fais.","J'aime qu'est-ce que tu fais.","J'aime ce qui tu fais."] | ["J'aime ce que tu fais."] | false | In the middle of a sentence, 'what' is ce que, never qu'est-ce que.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex009 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with compter in the present: Ce qui ___, c'est la vérité. | — | ["compte"] | false | Ce qui is singular, so the verb is in the il form: compte.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex010 | translate | Rewrite this sentence with Ce qui… c'est, to put la musique at the end: La musique me plaît. | — | ["Ce qui me plaît, c'est la musique"] | false | Ce qui replaces the subject, and c'est brings the important part to the end.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex011 | translate | Rewrite this sentence with Ce que… c'est, to put le marché at the end: J'adore le marché. | — | ["Ce que j'adore, c'est le marché"] | false | Le marché is the object of adore, so it's ce que.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex012 | translate | Write in French, to a friend: Everything you say is true. | — | ["Tout ce que tu dis est vrai"] | false | Tu is the subject of dis, so it's tout ce que.
- b1-ce-qui-ce-que-ex013 | reorder | Put the words in order: What surprises me is the price. | — | ["Ce qui me surprend, c'est le prix"] | false | Ce qui is the subject of surprend, and c'est introduces the important part.

## b1-relative-dont

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- dont | dohn | of which, about which, whose | —
- rêver de | reh-VAY duh | to dream of | —
- se souvenir de | suh soo-vuh-NEER duh | to remember | Like venir: je me souviens, nous nous souvenons.
- s'occuper de | soh-kew-PAY duh | to look after, to deal with | —
- se servir de | suh sehr-VEER duh | to use | Like partir: je me sers, nous nous servons.
- manquer de | mahn-KAY duh | to lack, to be short of | —
- se moquer de | suh moh-KAY duh | to make fun of | —
- avoir honte de | ah-vwahr OHNT duh | to be ashamed of | —
- être responsable de | eh-truh reh-spohn-SAH-bluh duh | to be in charge of | —
- le rêve | luh REV | the dream | —
- un outil | uh-noo-TEE | a tool | —
- un projet | uhn proh-ZHEH | a project, a plan | —
- une raison | ewn reh-ZOHN | a reason | —
- une époque | ew-nay-POK | a period, a time | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-relative-dont-ex001 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: C'est le livre ___ j'ai besoin. | — | ["dont"] | false | Avoir besoin takes de, so the relative pronoun is dont.
- b1-relative-dont-ex002 | multiple-choice | Choose the right word: C'est le film ___ je regarde. | ["que","dont","qui"] | ["que"] | false | Regarder takes a direct object, with no de, so it's que.
- b1-relative-dont-ex003 | multiple-choice | Choose the right word: C'est le film ___ tu parles. | ["dont","que","qui"] | ["dont"] | false | Parler de takes de, so it's dont.
- b1-relative-dont-ex004 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with se souvenir in the present: C'est une époque dont je me ___ bien. | — | ["souviens"] | false | Se souvenir works like venir: je me souviens.
- b1-relative-dont-ex005 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with se servir in the present: Voici l'outil dont je me ___. | — | ["sers"] | false | Servir works like partir: je sers, so je me sers.
- b1-relative-dont-ex006 | multiple-choice | Which sentence is correct? | ["C'est le livre dont j'ai besoin.","C'est le livre dont j'ai besoin de.","C'est le livre que j'ai besoin."] | ["C'est le livre dont j'ai besoin."] | false | Dont already contains the de, so you don't repeat it, and que can't replace de + a noun.
- b1-relative-dont-ex007 | multiple-choice | What does this mean? l'ami dont le père est médecin | ["the friend whose father is a doctor","the friend who is a doctor","the father whose friend is a doctor"] | ["the friend whose father is a doctor"] | false | Dont + le père means 'whose father'.
- b1-relative-dont-ex008 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (whose): J'ai une amie ___ le frère habite à Lyon. | — | ["dont"] | false | For 'whose', French uses dont + the article + the noun.
- b1-relative-dont-ex009 | multiple-choice | Choose the right word: C'est la ville ___ je rêve. | ["dont","que","qui"] | ["dont"] | false | Rêver takes de, so it's dont.
- b1-relative-dont-ex010 | translate | Join the two sentences with dont: C'est un projet. Je suis responsable de ce projet. | — | ["C'est un projet dont je suis responsable"] | false | Dont replaces de ce projet.
- b1-relative-dont-ex011 | translate | Join the two sentences with dont: J'ai un ami. Le père de cet ami est médecin. | — | ["J'ai un ami dont le père est médecin"] | false | Dont replaces de cet ami, and le père stays in front of the verb.
- b1-relative-dont-ex012 | translate | Write in French: It's the book I need. | — | ["C'est le livre dont j'ai besoin"] | false | Avoir besoin de, so dont.
- b1-relative-dont-ex013 | reorder | Put the words in order: It's a time I remember well. | — | ["C'est une époque dont je me souviens bien"] | false | Dont comes straight after the noun, then the usual order: subject, verb.

## b1-relationships

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- un ami proche / une amie proche | uh-nah-mee PROSH / ew-nah-mee PROSH | a close friend | —
- l'amitié | lah-mee-TYAY | friendship | Feminine: une amitié.
- se faire des amis | suh fehr day-zah-MEE | to make friends | —
- s'entendre bien avec | sahn-tahn-druh BYAN ah-vek | to get on well with | —
- faire confiance à | fehr kohn-FYAHNSS ah | to trust | —
- la confiance | lah kohn-FYAHNSS | trust | —
- l'amour | lah-MOOR | love | Masculine: un amour.
- un couple | uhn KOO-pluh | a couple | —
- être en couple | eh-truh ahn KOO-pluh | to be in a relationship | —
- un / une célibataire | uhn / ewn say-lee-bah-TEHR | a single person | Also an adjective: je suis célibataire.
- tomber amoureux / amoureuse de | tohn-bay ah-moo-RUH / ah-moo-RUHZ duh | to fall in love with | —
- se marier (avec) | suh mah-RYAY (ah-VEK) | to get married (to) | —
- le mariage | luh mah-RYAHZH | marriage, a wedding | —
- se disputer (avec) | suh dees-pew-TAY (ah-VEK) | to argue (with) | —
- une dispute | ewn dees-PEWT | an argument, a row | —
- un conflit | uhn kohn-FLEE | a conflict | —
- la jalousie | lah zhah-loo-ZEE | jealousy | —
- se réconcilier | suh ray-kohn-see-LYAY | to make up | —
- se séparer | suh say-pah-RAY | to separate, to split up | —
- rompre (avec) | ROHN-pruh (ah-VEK) | to break up (with) | Je romps, il rompt. Passé composé: j'ai rompu.
- divorcer | dee-vor-SAY | to get divorced | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-relationships-ex001 | multiple-choice | Which word means 'friendship'? | ["l'amitié","l'amour","la confiance"] | ["l'amitié"] | false | L'amitié is friendship. L'amour is love, and la confiance is trust.
- b1-relationships-ex002 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: Je m'entends bien ___ mes collègues. | — | ["avec"] | false | S'entendre bien avec quelqu'un: to get on well with someone.
- b1-relationships-ex003 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: Elle est tombée amoureuse ___ Paul. | — | ["de"] | false | French falls in love de someone, not 'with'.
- b1-relationships-ex004 | multiple-choice | Choose the right form of the passé composé: Ils ___ mariés en juin. | ["se sont","ont","s'ont"] | ["se sont"] | false | Se marier is reflexive, so it takes être, and the pronoun se stays: ils se sont mariés.
- b1-relationships-ex005 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with se disputer in the passé composé (Marc et Julie: a man and a woman): Marc et Julie se sont ___ hier. | — | ["disputés"] | true | With être, the participle agrees: a man and a woman together take the masculine plural, disputés.
- b1-relationships-ex006 | multiple-choice | Your friends argued, and now they're friends again. What happened? | ["Ils se sont réconciliés.","Ils se sont séparés.","Ils ont divorcé."] | ["Ils se sont réconciliés."] | false | Se réconcilier means to make up after an argument.
- b1-relationships-ex007 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with rompre in the passé composé: Il a ___ avec Anne. | — | ["rompu"] | false | The participle of rompre is rompu.
- b1-relationships-ex008 | multiple-choice | Someone who isn't in a relationship is… | ["célibataire","en couple","marié"] | ["célibataire"] | false | Célibataire means single.
- b1-relationships-ex009 | multiple-choice | Which verb means 'to break up'? | ["rompre","se réconcilier","se marier"] | ["rompre"] | false | Rompre (avec) is to break up (with). The other two are the opposite.
- b1-relationships-ex010 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with se séparer in the passé composé (nous: two women): Nous nous sommes ___. | — | ["séparées"] | false | With être, the participle agrees: two women take -ées.
- b1-relationships-ex011 | translate | Write in French: We get on well. | — | ["Nous nous entendons bien","On s'entend bien"] | false | S'entendre is reflexive: nous nous entendons, or on s'entend.
- b1-relationships-ex012 | translate | Write in French: They have been a couple for two years. | — | ["Ils sont en couple depuis deux ans"] | false | Something still true takes the present with depuis.
- b1-relationships-ex013 | translate | Write out this paragraph in the passé composé, filling each gap as shown (Paul et Léa: a man and a woman): Paul et Léa (se marier) en 2020. Ils (se disputer) une fois, mais ils (se réconcilier) le même jour. | — | ["Paul et Léa se sont mariés en 2020. Ils se sont disputés une fois, mais ils se sont réconciliés le même jour."] | true | All three verbs are reflexive, so they take être, and the participles agree in the masculine plural.
- b1-relationships-ex014 | reorder | Put the words in order: She fell in love with my brother. | — | ["Elle est tombée amoureuse de mon frère"] | false | Tomber takes être, so tombée and amoureuse both agree with elle.

## b1-review-opinions

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- un texte d'opinion | uhn tekst doh-pee-NYOHN | an opinion piece | —
- un lecteur / une lectrice | uhn lek-TUHR / ewn lek-TREESS | a reader | —
- réagir | ray-ah-ZHEER | to react | Like finir: je réagis, nous réagissons.
- convaincre | kohn-VAN-kruh | to convince | Je convaincs, il convainc, nous convainquons.
- convaincant / convaincante | kohn-van-KAHN / kohn-van-KAHNT | convincing | —
- de plus | duh PLEWSS | moreover, what's more | —
- par exemple | par eg-ZAHN-pluh | for example | —
- en conclusion | ahn kohn-klew-ZYOHN | in conclusion | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-review-opinions-ex001 | multiple-choice | Which phrase introduces an example? | ["Par exemple","De plus","En conclusion"] | ["Par exemple"] | false | Par exemple introduces an example. De plus adds a reason, and en conclusion ends the piece.
- b1-review-opinions-ex002 | multiple-choice | Which phrase adds a second reason? | ["De plus","Par exemple","En conclusion"] | ["De plus"] | false | De plus means 'moreover': it adds another reason.
- b1-review-opinions-ex003 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: Je pense ___ l'amitié est très importante. | — | ["que"] | false | Penser needs que before a whole opinion.
- b1-review-opinions-ex004 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (What): ___ compte en amitié, c'est la confiance. | — | ["Ce qui"] | false | Compte follows straight away, so it's ce qui.
- b1-review-opinions-ex005 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: C'est l'amie ___ j'ai besoin quand ça ne va pas. | — | ["dont"] | false | Avoir besoin takes de, so it's dont.
- b1-review-opinions-ex006 | multiple-choice | You want to tell a friend they're right. Choose the word: Tu as ___. | ["raison","tort","d'accord"] | ["raison"] | false | Avoir raison is to be right. Avoir tort is to be wrong, and d'accord goes with être.
- b1-review-opinions-ex007 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with se réconcilier in the passé composé (ils: two men): Ils se sont ___ hier. | — | ["réconciliés"] | false | With être, the participle agrees with ils: réconciliés.
- b1-review-opinions-ex008 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (convincing; ton texte: masculine): Ton texte est très ___. | — | ["convaincant"] | false | Convaincant agrees with texte, which is masculine.
- b1-review-opinions-ex009 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with convaincre in the present: Tu ne me ___ pas ! | — | ["convaincs"] | false | Convaincre: je convaincs, tu convaincs, il convainc.
- b1-review-opinions-ex010 | multiple-choice | Someone says « Je n'aime pas les textes d'opinion. » You do. What do you say? | ["Moi si.","Moi non plus.","Moi aussi."] | ["Moi si."] | false | Moi si contradicts a negative statement.
- b1-review-opinions-ex011 | translate | Write in French, replying to a writer you call vous: I don't agree with you, but that's a fair point. | — | ["Je ne suis pas d'accord avec vous, mais ce n'est pas faux"] | false | Disagree with je ne suis pas d'accord, then concede with ce n'est pas faux.
- b1-review-opinions-ex012 | translate | Write out this conclusion, filling each gap as shown: (In conclusion), (in my opinion) (what matters) en amitié, c'est la confiance. | — | ["En conclusion, à mon avis, ce qui compte en amitié, c'est la confiance."] | true | En conclusion, then an opinion marker, then ce qui… c'est to put the key word last.
- b1-review-opinions-ex013 | translate | Write in French: For example, what I like is the market. | — | ["Par exemple, ce que j'aime, c'est le marché"] | false | J'aime has a subject, so it's ce que.
- b1-review-opinions-ex014 | reorder | Put the words in order: What's more, I get on well with my colleagues. | — | ["De plus, je m'entends bien avec mes collègues"] | false | De plus starts the sentence, then the reflexive verb s'entendre.

## b1-si-present-futur

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- si | see | if | A new meaning: you know si as 'yes' after a negative question.
- s'il / s'ils | seel | if he, if it / if they | —
- sinon | see-NOHN | otherwise, or else | —
- rater | rah-TAY | to miss (a train, a bus) | —
- prévoir | pray-VWAHR | to plan, to forecast | Like voir: je prévois, nous prévoyons. Participle: prévu.
- reporter | ruh-por-TAY | to postpone | —
- un imprévu | uh-nan-pray-VEW | something unexpected | —
- un parapluie | uhn pah-rah-PLWEE | an umbrella | —
- un bouchon | uhn boo-SHOHN | a traffic jam | —
- à l'heure | ah LUHR | on time | —
- en avance | ah-nah-VAHNSS | early | —
- tant pis | tahn PEE | never mind, too bad | —
- tant mieux | tahn MYUH | so much the better, good | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-si-present-futur-ex001 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with pleuvoir in the present: S'il ___, on restera à la maison. | — | ["pleut"] | false | After si, use the present: s'il pleut.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex002 | multiple-choice | Choose the right form: Si tu viens, je ___ content. | ["serai","sera","serons"] | ["serai"] | false | The other half takes the futur, and je takes serai.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex003 | multiple-choice | Which sentence is correct? | ["S'il fait beau, on ira à la plage.","Si il fait beau, on ira à la plage.","S'il fera beau, on ira à la plage."] | ["S'il fait beau, on ira à la plage."] | false | Si becomes s' before il, and the verb after si is in the present.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex004 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with appeler in the imperative (vous): Si vous êtes en retard, ___-moi. | — | ["appelez"] | false | Si + present can be followed by a command: appelez-moi.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex005 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with si, shortened if needed: ___ ils viennent, nous ferons un gâteau. | — | ["S'"] | false | Si becomes s' before ils, just as before il.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex006 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (otherwise): Pars maintenant, ___ tu vas rater le train. | — | ["sinon"] | false | Sinon means 'otherwise'.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex007 | multiple-choice | Something unexpected has come up. It's… | ["un imprévu","un parapluie","un bouchon"] | ["un imprévu"] | false | Un imprévu is something you didn't plan for.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex008 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with reporter in the futur (on): S'il y a un imprévu, on ___ le dîner. | — | ["reportera"] | false | On takes the il form of the futur: reportera.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex009 | multiple-choice | A friend can't come tonight. You don't mind much. What do you say? | ["Tant pis !","Tant mieux !","Sinon !"] | ["Tant pis !"] | false | Tant pis means 'never mind'. Tant mieux would mean you're glad.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex010 | translate | Write in French: If it rains, we'll stay at home. | — | ["S'il pleut, nous resterons à la maison","S'il pleut, on restera à la maison","Nous resterons à la maison s'il pleut","On restera à la maison s'il pleut"] | true | Si + present, then the futur. Either half can come first.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex011 | translate | Write in French, to a friend: If you're tired, rest. | — | ["Si tu es fatigué, repose-toi","Si tu es fatiguée, repose-toi"] | false | Si + present, then a command. Reposer is reflexive, so it's repose-toi.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex012 | translate | Write out these plans, filling each gap as shown (on = we): S'il (faire, present) beau, on (aller, futur) à la plage. Si le train (être, present) en retard, on (prendre, futur) le bus. Sinon, on (arriver, futur) à l'heure. | — | ["S'il fait beau, on ira à la plage. Si le train est en retard, on prendra le bus. Sinon, on arrivera à l'heure."] | true | The present after si, the futur in the other half, and sinon for the other case.
- b1-si-present-futur-ex013 | reorder | Put the words in order: If you leave now, you'll be early. | — | ["Si tu pars maintenant, tu seras en avance"] | false | Si + present first, then the futur.

## b1-conditionnel-present

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- je parlerais / tu parlerais / il parlerait | zhuh par-luh-REH / tew par-luh-REH / eel par-luh-REH | I / you / he would speak | —
- nous parlerions / vous parleriez / ils parleraient | noo par-luh-RYOHN / voo par-luh-RYAY / eel par-luh-REH | we / you / they would speak | —
- je serais | zhuh suh-REH | I would be | —
- j'aurais | zhoh-REH | I would have | —
- j'irais | zhee-REH | I would go | —
- je ferais | zhuh fuh-REH | I would do, I would make | —
- il pourrait | eel poo-REH | he could, he might | —
- je devrais / tu devrais | zhuh duh-VREH / tew duh-VREH | I / you should | —
- je saurais | zhuh soh-REH | I would know | —
- je viendrais | zhuh vyan-DREH | I would come | —
- il faudrait | eel foh-DREH | we'd need to, someone should | —
- j'aimerais | zheh-muh-REH | I'd like | —
- je préférerais | zhuh pray-fay-ruh-REH | I'd prefer, I'd rather | —
- ce serait | suh suh-REH | it would be | —
- possible | poh-SEE-bluh | possible | —
- selon | suh-LOHN | according to | —
- il paraît que | eel pah-reh KUH | apparently, it seems that | —
- apparemment | ah-pah-rah-MAHN | apparently | —
- un / une ministre | uhn / ewn mee-NEES-truh | a minister | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-conditionnel-present-ex001 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with vouloir in the conditionnel: Je ___ un café, s'il vous plaît. | — | ["voudrais"] | false | Vouloir has the stem voudr-, plus -ais.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex002 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with aimer in the conditionnel: Nous ___ partir en mai. | — | ["aimerions"] | false | The stem aimer- plus the nous ending -ions.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex003 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with pouvoir in the conditionnel: Tu ___ m'aider ? | — | ["pourrais"] | false | Pouvoir has the stem pourr-, with two r's.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex004 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with être in the conditionnel: Ce ___ possible ? | — | ["serait"] | false | Être has the stem ser-, and ce takes the il ending -ait.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex005 | multiple-choice | Which ending does the conditionnel use with ils? | ["-aient","-ont","-ez"] | ["-aient"] | false | The conditionnel uses the imparfait endings, so ils takes -aient. -ont is the futur ending.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex006 | multiple-choice | Choose the form that means 'I would go': Avec plus de temps, j'___ à Lyon. | ["irais","irai","allais"] | ["irais"] | false | Irais is the conditionnel (would go). Irai is the futur (will go), and allais is the imparfait (was going).
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex007 | multiple-choice | Which sentence reports news that isn't confirmed? | ["Selon Paul, le restaurant serait fermé.","Le restaurant est fermé.","Le restaurant sera fermé."] | ["Selon Paul, le restaurant serait fermé."] | false | Selon + the conditionnel presents something you've heard but can't confirm.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex008 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with falloir in the conditionnel: Il ___ partir tôt. | — | ["faudrait"] | false | Falloir has the stem faudr-: il faudrait.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex009 | multiple-choice | Which one is the futur, not the conditionnel? | ["je parlerai","je parlerais","il parlerait"] | ["je parlerai"] | false | The futur ends in -ai with je. The conditionnel ends in -ais or -ait.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex010 | translate | Write in French: I would like to leave in May. | — | ["J'aimerais partir en mai","Je voudrais partir en mai"] | false | J'aimerais or je voudrais, then the infinitive.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex011 | translate | Rewrite this request politely, with pouvoir in the conditionnel: Vous pouvez répéter ? | — | ["Vous pourriez répéter ?","Pourriez-vous répéter ?"] | false | Pouvez becomes pourriez, which softens the request.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex012 | translate | Rewrite this sentence politely, with vouloir in the conditionnel: Je veux une chambre pour deux nuits. | — | ["Je voudrais une chambre pour deux nuits"] | false | Je veux sounds demanding. Je voudrais is the polite form.
- b1-conditionnel-present-ex013 | reorder | Put the words in order: It would be a good idea. | — | ["Ce serait une bonne idée"] | false | Ce serait: it would be.

## b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- gagner au loto | gah-nyay oh loh-TOH | to win the lottery | —
- riche | reesh | rich | —
- la célébrité | lah say-lay-bree-TAY | fame | —
- au bord de la mer | oh bor duh lah MEHR | by the sea | —
- avoir le temps | ah-vwahr luh TAHN | to have time | —
- changer de vie | shahn-zhay duh VEE | to change your life | —
- déménager | day-may-nah-ZHAY | to move house | —
- imaginer | ee-mah-zhee-NAY | to imagine | —
- supposer | sew-poh-ZAY | to suppose | —
- un superpouvoir | uhn sew-pehr-poo-VWAHR | a superpower | —
- invisible | an-vee-ZEE-bluh | invisible | —
- voler | voh-LAY | to fly | —
- si j'étais toi | see zhay-teh TWAH | if I were you | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex001 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with avoir in the imparfait: Si j'___ le temps, je voyagerais. | — | ["avais"] | false | After si, an imagined situation takes the imparfait.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex002 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with faire in the conditionnel: Si tu gagnais au loto, qu'est-ce que tu ___ ? | — | ["ferais"] | false | The result of an imagined situation takes the conditionnel.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex003 | multiple-choice | Which sentence is correct? | ["Si j'étais riche, j'achèterais une maison.","Si je serais riche, j'achèterais une maison.","Si j'étais riche, j'achèterai une maison."] | ["Si j'étais riche, j'achèterais une maison."] | false | Imparfait after si, conditionnel in the other half.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex004 | multiple-choice | Which sentence talks about a real possibility? | ["S'il fait beau demain, on ira à la plage.","S'il faisait beau, on irait à la plage.","Si j'étais toi, j'irais à la plage."] | ["S'il fait beau demain, on ira à la plage."] | false | Si + present and the futur are for things that may really happen.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex005 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with habiter in the conditionnel: Nous ___ au bord de la mer si nous étions riches. | — | ["habiterions"] | false | The stem habiter- plus -ions.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex006 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with avoir in the imparfait: S'ils ___ plus de temps, ils viendraient. | — | ["avaient"] | false | Ils avaient, in the imparfait after si.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex007 | multiple-choice | Choose the form that means 'I would stay': Si j'étais toi, je ___ à la maison. | ["resterais","resterai","restais"] | ["resterais"] | false | Resterais is the conditionnel. Resterai is the futur, and restais is the imparfait.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex008 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with pouvoir in the imparfait: Si elle ___ voler, elle viendrait au travail en cinq minutes. | — | ["pouvait"] | false | Elle pouvait, in the imparfait after si.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex009 | multiple-choice | Which verb means 'to move house'? | ["déménager","voler","imaginer"] | ["déménager"] | false | Déménager means to move to a new home.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex010 | translate | Write in French: If I had time, I would travel. | — | ["Si j'avais le temps, je voyagerais","Je voyagerais si j'avais le temps"] | false | Si + imparfait, then the conditionnel. Either half can come first.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex011 | translate | Write in French, to a friend: What would you do if you were invisible? | — | ["Qu'est-ce que tu ferais si tu étais invisible ?","Que ferais-tu si tu étais invisible ?"] | false | The conditionnel ferais, then si + the imparfait étais.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex012 | translate | Write out these sentences, filling each gap as shown: Si j'(être, imparfait) riche, j'(acheter, conditionnel) une maison au bord de la mer. Si j'(avoir, imparfait) un superpouvoir, je (voler, conditionnel). Si j'(être, imparfait) toi, je (changer, conditionnel) de vie. | — | ["Si j'étais riche, j'achèterais une maison au bord de la mer. Si j'avais un superpouvoir, je volerais. Si j'étais toi, je changerais de vie."] | false | Each sentence pairs si + the imparfait with the conditionnel.
- b1-si-imparfait-conditionnel-ex013 | reorder | Put the words in order: If I were you, I would move house. | — | ["Si j'étais toi, je déménagerais"] | false | Si j'étais toi, then the conditionnel.

## b1-giving-advice

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- tu devrais / vous devriez | tew duh-VREH / voo duh-vree-YAY | you should | —
- à ta place / à votre place | ah tah PLASS / ah voh-truh PLASS | if I were you, in your place | —
- ce serait une bonne idée de | suh suh-reh ewn bonn ee-DAY duh | it would be a good idea to | —
- je te conseille de / je vous conseille de | zhuh tuh kohn-SEH-y duh / zhuh voo kohn-SEH-y duh | I advise you to | —
- il vaudrait mieux | eel voh-dreh MYUH | it would be better to | Followed by an infinitive.
- n'hésite pas à / n'hésitez pas à | nay-zeet pah ZAH / nay-zee-tay pah ZAH | don't hesitate to, feel free to | —
- ne pas + infinitive | nuh PAH | not to (essaie de ne pas oublier: try not to forget) | —
- conseiller | kohn-seh-YAY | to advise | —
- un conseil | uhn kohn-SEH-y | a piece of advice | Countable: des conseils (some advice).
- demander conseil à | duh-mahn-day kohn-SEH-y ah | to ask (someone) for advice | —
- suivre un conseil | swee-vruh uhn kohn-SEH-y | to follow a piece of advice | Je suis, tu suis, il suit, nous suivons.
- une solution | ewn soh-lew-SYOHN | a solution | —
- Je vais essayer. | zhuh veh zeh-seh-YAY | I'll try. | —
- le stress | luh STRESS | stress | —
- se détendre | suh day-TAHN-druh | to relax | —
- le sommeil | luh soh-MEH-y | sleep | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-giving-advice-ex001 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with devoir in the conditionnel: Tu ___ dormir plus. | — | ["devrais"] | false | Tu devrais (you should) + infinitive.
- b1-giving-advice-ex002 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with devoir in the conditionnel: Vous ___ parler à votre médecin. | — | ["devriez"] | false | With vous, it's vous devriez.
- b1-giving-advice-ex003 | fill-in | Fill in the gap (in your place): À ta ___, je partirais. | — | ["place"] | false | À ta place, then the conditionnel.
- b1-giving-advice-ex004 | multiple-choice | You're advising someone you call vous. Which sentence fits? | ["Je vous conseille de vous reposer.","Repose-toi !","Je te conseille de te reposer."] | ["Je vous conseille de vous reposer."] | false | With vous, keep vous all the way through: je vous conseille de vous reposer.
- b1-giving-advice-ex005 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: Je te conseille ___ ne pas boire de café le soir. | — | ["de"] | false | Conseiller takes de before the infinitive.
- b1-giving-advice-ex006 | multiple-choice | Which sentence is correct? | ["Essaie de ne pas oublier.","Essaie de pas oublier.","Essaie de ne oublier pas."] | ["Essaie de ne pas oublier."] | false | With an infinitive, ne and pas go together, before the verb.
- b1-giving-advice-ex007 | fill-in | Fill in the gap with valoir in the conditionnel: Il ___ mieux partir tôt. | — | ["vaudrait"] | false | Il vaudrait mieux + infinitive: it would be better to.
- b1-giving-advice-ex008 | multiple-choice | A friend gives you advice you like. What do you say? | ["Bonne idée, je vais essayer.","Tu as tort.","Pas du tout."] | ["Bonne idée, je vais essayer."] | false | Je vais essayer accepts the advice.
- b1-giving-advice-ex009 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: N'hésite pas ___ m'appeler. | — | ["à"] | true | Hésiter takes à before an infinitive.
- b1-giving-advice-ex010 | translate | Write in French, to a friend: If I were you, I would sleep more. | — | ["Si j'étais toi, je dormirais plus","À ta place, je dormirais plus"] | true | Si j'étais toi or à ta place, then the conditionnel.
- b1-giving-advice-ex011 | translate | Write in French, to a friend: Try not to forget your keys. | — | ["Essaie de ne pas oublier tes clés"] | false | Essayer de, then ne pas together before the infinitive.
- b1-giving-advice-ex012 | translate | Write out this reply to someone you call vous, filling each gap as shown: (You should) parler à votre directeur. (In your place), je (ne pas travailler, conditionnel) le soir. (I advise you) de faire du sport. | — | ["Vous devriez parler à votre directeur. À votre place, je ne travaillerais pas le soir. Je vous conseille de faire du sport."] | true | Vous devriez, à votre place + conditionnel, and je vous conseille de + infinitive.
- b1-giving-advice-ex013 | reorder | Put the words in order: It would be better to rest. | — | ["Il vaudrait mieux se reposer"] | false | Il vaudrait mieux + infinitive.

## b1-dreams-and-ambitions

### Vocab

fr | pron | en | note

- avoir l'intention de | ah-vwahr lan-tahn-SYOHN duh | to intend to | —
- compter + infinitive | kohn-TAY | to plan to, to intend to | —
- un but | uhn BEWT | a goal, an aim | Mon but est de… (My aim is to…)
- une ambition | ew-nahn-bee-SYOHN | an ambition | —
- espérer | ess-pay-RAY | to hope | J'espère, nous espérons.
- motivé / motivée | moh-tee-VAY | motivated | —
- réaliser un rêve | ray-ah-lee-zay uhn REV | to make a dream come true | —
- dans l'idéal | dahn lee-day-AHL | ideally | —
- idéal / idéale | ee-day-AHL | ideal | —
- l'avenir | lahv-NEER | the future | Masculine: un bel avenir.
- à l'avenir | ah lahv-NEER | in future | —
- une carrière | ewn kah-RYEHR | a career | —
- créer | kray-AY | to create | —
- monter sa propre entreprise | mohn-tay sah proh-prahn-truh-PREEZ | to set up your own business | —
- faire du bénévolat | fehr dew bay-nay-voh-LAH | to do voluntary work | —
- la retraite | lah ruh-TRET | retirement | —
- prendre sa retraite | prahn-druh sah ruh-TRET | to retire | —

### Exercises

id | type | prompt | options | answers | strictAccents | explanation

- b1-dreams-and-ambitions-ex001 | multiple-choice | Which sentence is the most certain plan? | ["Je vais partir en juin.","J'aimerais partir en juin.","Si je pouvais, je partirais en juin."] | ["Je vais partir en juin."] | false | Aller + infinitive is a firm plan. The other two are wishes.
- b1-dreams-and-ambitions-ex002 | fill-in | Fill in the gap: J'ai l'intention ___ changer de travail. | — | ["de"] | false | Avoir l'intention de + infinitive.
